## 例 688：World Cup Bobblehead Figure

**来源：** [@SaasJunctionHQ](https://x.com/SaasJunctionHQ/status/2070943717238919211) / [awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts](https://github.com/EvoLinkAI/awesome-gpt-image-2-prompts)

![case688.jpg](images/case688.jpg)

```text
Turn any player into a collectible.

Generate a photorealistic studio image of a custom bobble-head collectible figure modeled after [PLAYER NAME], based entirely on the attached reference photo. The reference image is the absolute and sole source of truth for all physical characteristics — do not rely on any prior knowledge, training data, or assumptions about how [PLAYER NAME] looks. Every visual decision must be driven exclusively by what is visible in the attached photo.
Follow all of the rules below without exception:
Likeness & Identity
•Engrave the name ”[PLAYER NAME]” in clean white lettering on the front face of the two-tiered base beneath the figure.
•Reproduce every distinctive facial feature — bone structure, eye shape, nose, lips, skin tone, and complexion — exactly as seen in the reference. Do not smooth, soften, generalize, or idealize any feature.
•The hairstyle must match the reference with exact fidelity — preserve natural curl, wave, coil, frizz, volume, length, and direction. Never straighten, flatten, or restyle the hair.
•Replicate facial hair (beard, mustache, stubble) at the precise density and pattern shown in the reference. If the subject appears clean-shaven, add absolutely no facial hair.
•Include accessories such as glasses, earrings, or a hat only if they are visibly present in the reference photo. If no accessories appear, add none whatsoever.
Figure Design & Style
•The figure must follow a classic sporty bobble-head aesthetic — proportionally large head, compact body, and an overall collectible toy feel. This is not a Funko Pop, not a caricature, and not a hyper-stylized vinyl figure.
•The neck must be short and thick in proportion, with absolutely no spring, coil, or hinge mechanism visible.
•Dress the figure in the official national football kit that corresponds to [PLAYER NAME]’s country — use the correct jersey color, shorts, and socks as worn by their national team. Do not default to any generic or incorrect kit.
Pose & Base
•Pose the figure standing upright with one foot casually resting on top of a standard soccer ball, which sits directly on the base.
•The base must be two-tiered, with a top surface that mimics the texture of matte natural grass — no gloss, no shine, no reflection of any kind.
Background & Lighting
•The background must show a football stadium that is softly blurred using a shallow depth-of-field bokeh effect, so the figure remains the clear focal point.
•The stadium lighting must evoke a warm golden hour — rich amber and soft orange tones, low sun angle, cinematic warmth.
Format
•Final output must be in 4:5 portrait orientation.
```

## 例 693：柑橘果园SOLARA品牌奢华时尚大片

**来源：** [@ou_zhen599](https://x.com/ou_zhen599/status/2093396223055286321) / [OpenNana](https://opennana.com/awesome-prompt-gallery/solara-luxury-fashion-editorial-orchard-photography)

![case693.jpg](images/case693.jpg)

```text
Create a Cannes-level luxury fashion editorial image for an original womenswear brand named "SOLARA". Use 100% live-action ultra-realistic photography. Preserve the exact structural logic of the scene: a tall vertical composition inside a dense citrus orchard corridor, one adult female model walking or gliding forward through the center path as the absolute visual hero, surrounded tightly on both sides by lush orange trees loaded with ripe fruit, with a deep teal-blue sky above and strong natural sunlight casting dramatic leaf shadows across her body and dress. The image must feel like a high-fashion magazine cover without any visible text: bold, elegant, sensual, Mediterranean, and unforgettable.

Style lock: ultra-real luxury fashion editorial photography, premium magazine-cover energy, natural outdoor couture campaign, vivid sunlit realism, no illustration, no CGI look, no plastic beauty retouching, no artificial fantasy rendering.

Core composition: full-body female model centered slightly forward in the frame, photographed at a slightly low-to-mid angle, walking or pausing mid-step through a narrow orchard path. Trees and fruit must form a natural vertical tunnel around her, framing the figure symmetrically but organically. The dress occupies a large portion of the lower frame with strong flowing movement. Keep the model dominant, with the orchard acting as a rich but subordinate environment.

Wardrobe design: a striking saturated orange-red luxury dress with a deep V neckline, elegant wrapped bodice, defined waist, wide statement belt, and a long flowing pleated skirt. The fabric must feel lightweight, refined, and expensive, with enough motion to create sculptural folds and flowing volume near the hem. The dress color should harmonize with the oranges while still remaining the primary visual statement through stronger saturation, cleaner fabric sheen, and larger uninterrupted shape.

Lighting: strong natural sunlight with crisp but beautiful shadow structure. Sunlight should illuminate the face, collarbone, shoulders, waist, and skirt folds while leaf shadows move across the dress and skin in a cinematic pattern. Maintain controlled contrast: bright highlights, rich midtones, and clean readable shadows. No muddy dark areas, no dead blacks, no haze that weakens the image. The scene must feel sun-drenched, sharp, and luxurious.

Model realism: adult female anatomy must be fully natural, elegant, and anatomically correct. Real human skin only: fine visible pores, subtle facial peach fuzz, natural skin microtexture, realistic lip texture, soft semi-matte skin sheen, natural tone variation, delicate capillary warmth, slight joint pigmentation, realistic subcutaneous volume, faint vellus hair, authentic photographic detail. Natural mature feminine chest structure with believable softness and gravity. Correct shoulders, arms, hands, waist, pelvis, thighs, knees, calves, ankles, and feet. No mannequin effect, no over-smoothing, no distorted limbs or fingers.

Face and hair: refined adult face with calm self-possessed expression, slightly lifted chin, softly closed or half-closed eyes or a serene editorial gaze, natural brows, realistic nose bridge, softly defined cheekbones, and bold but elegant warm red-orange lips. Hair styled into a clean elevated bun or sleek updo with a few controlled loose wisps. Expression should feel proud, sunlit, and fashion-forward.

Environment and details: abundant ripe oranges, glossy dark green leaves, realistic branch density, sunlit fruit highlights, soft depth blur in foreground leaves and fruit near the frame edges, and a narrow earthy path below. The orchard should feel immersive and high-end, not rustic or messy. Use selective depth of field so some foreground fruit and leaves softly blur while the model remains tack sharp.

Color strategy: dominant palette of vivid orange-red, ripe mandarin orange, deep botanical green, golden sunlight, warm skin tones, and a saturated teal-blue sky. The dress and lips should create the strongest warm focal point, while the fruit and foliage echo and amplify the palette.

Art direction goals: model-first hierarchy, magazine-cover impact, intense color harmony, strong natural shadow play, premium Mediterranean glamour, clean luxury realism, no text, no logo, no watermark, no extra props, no clutter, no black blotches, no muddy tones, no cheap commercial fashion look.

Negative prompt: text, typography, logo, watermark, magazine masthead, copied headline, copied website text, AI look, CGI skin, plastic skin, waxy skin, poreless face, over-retouched beauty filter, extra fingers, missing fingers, twisted limbs, broken anatomy, distorted hands, synthetic fabric, muddy shadows, dead-black patches, black blotches, cluttered orchard, low-resolution oranges, fake leaves, flat lighting, cheap catalog fashion look, cartoon rendering, surreal proportions, blurred face.
```

A photorealistic cinematic portrait of a young East Asian woman in her early twenties, around 20 years old, seated outdoors beside a modern architectural structure clad in small orange-red tiles. She has fair, smooth skin and a delicate oval face with soft feminine features. Her eyes are dark brown, large and slightly downturned, with a calm, distant gaze directed off to the left of the camera rather than straight into the lens. Her eyebrows are fine and natural, her nose is small and refined, and her lips are softly full with a neutral, slightly pensive expression. Her hair is a sleek short black bob cut, ending around the jawline, with full straight bangs covering the forehead and framing her face neatly.

Her legs are extended forward and angled toward the camera, creating a strong perspective with her legs and high heels prominent in the foreground. She is wearing a dark navy or black low-cut spaghetti-strap dress with a textured flowing skirt and a revealing neckline, layered with a loose soft cardigan draped off her shoulders in a pale blue-and-white fuzzy knit. She also wears black open-toe high-heeled sandals with thin ankle straps and a delicate minimal necklace.

The foreground shows green grass and a bit of pavement, while the background remains softly detailed and natural.

Use soft diffused natural lighting from a cloudy day, creating gentle shadows and smooth skin tones. Capture the image with a slightly low angle and a moderate wide-to-standard lens look, around 35mm to 50mm, emphasizing her legs in the foreground while keeping her face and upper body as the emotional focal point.
